TGLV354.TraceCompletion.ShiftRelated.{u_1} {A : Type u_1} [Monoid.{u_1} A] (a b : A) : Prop
def TGLV354.TraceCompletion.ShiftRelated.{u_1} : {A : Type u_1} → [Monoid.{u_1} A] → A → A → Prop :=
fun {A} [Monoid.{u_1} A] a b =>
  ∃ n,
    LT.lt.{0} 0 n ∧
      ∃ r s,
        Eq.{u_1 + 1} (HMul.hMul.{u_1, u_1, u_1} a r) (HMul.hMul.{u_1, u_1, u_1} r b) ∧
          Eq.{u_1 + 1} (HMul.hMul.{u_1, u_1, u_1} s a) (HMul.hMul.{u_1, u_1, u_1} b s) ∧
            Eq.{u_1 + 1} (HPow.hPow.{u_1, 0, u_1} a n) (HMul.hMul.{u_1, u_1, u_1} r s) ∧
              Eq.{u_1 + 1} (HPow.hPow.{u_1, 0, u_1} b n) (HMul.hMul.{u_1, u_1, u_1} s r)

        (Exists.intro.{u_1 + 1} y
          ⟨of_eq_true

                  (eq_self.{u_1 + 1} (HMul.hMul.{u_1, u_1, u_1} y (HMul.hMul.{u_1, u_1, u_1} x y)))),

theorem TGLV354.TraceCompletion.ShiftRelated.map.{u_1, u_2} : ∀ {A : Type u_1} [inst : Monoid.{u_1} A] {a b : A}
  {B : Type u_2} [inst_1 : Monoid.{u_2} B] (f : MonoidHom.{u_1, u_2} A B),
  TGLV354.TraceCompletion.ShiftRelated.{u_1} a b → TGLV354.TraceCompletion.ShiftRelated.{u_2} (f a) (f b) :=

  Exists.casesOn.{1} h fun n h =>
    And.casesOn.{0} h fun hn right =>
      Exists.casesOn.{u_1 + 1} right fun r h =>
        Exists.casesOn.{u_1 + 1} h fun s h =>
          And.casesOn.{0} h fun hr right =>
            And.casesOn.{0} right fun hs right =>
              And.casesOn.{0} right fun ha hb =>
                Exists.intro.{1} n
                  ⟨hn,
                    Exists.intro.{u_2 + 1} (f r)
                      (Exists.intro.{u_2 + 1} (f s)
                        ⟨Eq.mp.{0}

                            (congrArg.{u_1 + 1, u_2 + 1} (⇑f) hr),
                          ⟨Eq.mp.{0}

                                (congrArg.{u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1} Eq.{u_2 + 1} (map_mul.{u_1, u_2, max u_1 u_2} f s a))
                                (map_mul.{u_1, u_2, max u_1 u_2} f b s))
                              (congrArg.{u_1 + 1, u_2 + 1} (⇑f) hs),

theorem TGLV354.TraceCompletion.positiveShift_trace_compatible : ∀ (P : TGLExt.SiteProfile)
  (p q : TGLV350.Regular.PositiveCoreInput P),
  TGLV354.TraceCompletion.ShiftRelated.{0} (Subtype.mk.{1} ↑p ⋯) (Subtype.mk.{1} ↑q ⋯) →

    And.casesOn.{0} h fun hn right =>
      Exists.casesOn.{1} right fun r h =>
        Exists.casesOn.{1} h fun s h =>
          And.casesOn.{0} h fun hr right =>
            And.casesOn.{0} right fun left right =>
              And.casesOn.{0} right fun hp hq =>
                have hr' := congrArg.{1, 1} (fun x => ↑x) hr;
                have hp' := congrArg.{1, 1} (fun x => ↑x) hp;
                have hq' := congrArg.{1, 1} (fun x => ↑x) hq;

                  TGLV354.TraceCompletion.boundedPolar_transports_shift_factors (↑p) (↑q) (↑r) (↑s)

                    (TGLV354.boundedPolar_mem (TGLV350.Regular.regularCoreAlgebra P) (↑r) (Subtype.property.{1} r));
                TGLV354.TraceCompletion.scalarTrace_transport_of_support P p q u hpol.left hpol.right

TGLV354.TraceCompletion.scalarTrace_transport_of_support (P : TGLExt.SiteProfile)

  (htransport : Eq.{1} (↑p) (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} ↑u ↑q) (star.{0} ↑u)))

theorem TGLV354.TraceCompletion.scalarTrace_transport_of_support : ∀ (P : TGLExt.SiteProfile)
  (p q : TGLV350.Regular.PositiveCoreInput P)
  (u : ↥(VonNeumannAlgebra.toStarSubalgebra.{0} (TGLV350.Regular.regularCoreAlgebra P))),
  Eq.{1} (↑p) (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} ↑u ↑q) (star.{0} ↑u)) →
    Eq.{1} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (star.{0} ↑u) ↑u) ↑q) ↑q →
      Eq.{1} (TGLV350.Regular.scalarInverseLimitWeight P p) (TGLV350.Regular.scalarInverseLimitWeight P q) :=
fun P p q u htransport hsupport =>
  Exists.casesOn.{1}
    (TGLV354.TraceCompletion.transport_square_pair✝ (TGLV350.Regular.regularCoreAlgebra P)
      (Subtype.mk.{1} (↑p) (Subtype.property.{1} p).left) (Subtype.mk.{1} (↑q) (Subtype.property.{1} q).left) u
      (Subtype.property.{1} q).right htransport hsupport)
    fun a h =>
    And.casesOn.{0} h fun haa haa' =>
      have hq := Subtype.ext.{1} haa;
      have hp :=
        Subtype.ext.{1}
          (Eq.mpr.{0}
            (id.{0}
              (congrFun'.{1, 1}
                (congrArg.{1, 1} Eq.{1}
                  (congrArg.{1, 1} Subtype.val.{1}
                    (Subtype.mk.congr_simp.{1} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (star.{0} ↑(star.{0} a)) ↑(star.{0} a))
                      (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑a) (star.{0} ↑a))
                      (congrFun'.{1, 1} (congrArg.{1, 1} HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (star_star.{0} ↑a)) (star.{0} ↑a))
                      (TGLV351.positiveSquare._proof_1 P (star.{0} a)))))
                ↑p))
            haa');
      have ht := TGLV350.Regular.scalarInverseLimitWeight_tracial P a;
      Eq.symm.{1}
        (Eq.mp.{0}
          (congrArg.{1, 1}
            (fun _a =>
              Eq.{1} (TGLV350.Regular.scalarInverseLimitWeight P q) (TGLV350.Regular.scalarInverseLimitWeight P _a))
            hp)

theorem TGLV354.TraceCompletion.selfAdjoint_pow_closedRange.{u_1} : ∀ {H : Type u_1} [inst : NormedAddCommGroup.{u_1} H]
  [inst_1 : InnerProductSpace.{0, u_1} ℂ H] [inst_2 : CompleteSpace.{u_1} H]
  (A : ContinuousLinearMap.{0, 0, u_1, u_1} (RingHom.id.{0} ℂ) H H),
  IsSelfAdjoint.{u_1} A →
    ∀ (n : ℕ),
      LT.lt.{0} 0 n →
        Eq.{u_1 + 1}
          (Submodule.topologicalClosure.{0, u_1} (LinearMap.range.{0, 0, u_1, u_1} ↑(HPow.hPow.{u_1, 0, u_1} A n)))
          (Submodule.topologicalClosure.{0, u_1} (LinearMap.range.{0, 0, u_1, u_1} ↑A)) :=
fun {H} [NormedAddCommGroup.{u_1} H] [InnerProductSpace.{0, u_1} ℂ H] [CompleteSpace.{u_1} H] A hA n hn =>
  have h := ContinuousLinearMap.orthogonal_ker.{0, u_1, u_1} (HPow.hPow.{u_1, 0, u_1} A n);
  have h' := ContinuousLinearMap.orthogonal_ker.{0, u_1, u_1} A;
  Eq.trans.{u_1 + 1}
    (Eq.symm.{u_1 + 1}
      (Eq.mp.{0}
        (congrArg.{u_1 + 1, 1}

TGLV354.projection_join_comparison {H : Type} [NormedAddCommGroup.{0} H] [InnerProductSpace.{0, 0} ℂ H]

theorem TGLV354.projection_join_comparison : ∀ {H : Type} [inst : NormedAddCommGroup.{0} H]
  [inst_1 : InnerProductSpace.{0, 0} ℂ H] [inst_2 : CompleteSpace.{0} H] (N : VonNeumannAlgebra.{0} H)
  (p q : TGLV354.CoreProjection N),
  ∃ u,
    Membership.mem.{0, 0} N u ∧
      LE.le.{0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (star.{0} u) u) p.operator ∧
        Eq.{1} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} u (star.{0} u)) (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} (p ⊔ q).operator q.operator) :=
fun {H} [NormedAddCommGroup.{0} H] [InnerProductSpace.{0, 0} ℂ H] [CompleteSpace.{0} H] N p q =>
  let P := p.operator;
  let Q := q.operator;
  let R := (p ⊔ q).operator;
  let A := HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} 1 Q) P;
  let F := HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} R Q;
  have hp := isStarProjection_starProjection.{0, 0};
  have hq := isStarProjection_starProjection.{0, 0};
  have hr := isStarProjection_starProjection.{0, 0};
  have hPR :=
    (IsStarProjection.le_iff_mul_eq_left.{0} hp hr).mp
      ((TGLV354.CoreProjection.operator_le_iff p (p ⊔ q)).mpr le_sup_left.{0});
  have hQR :=
    (IsStarProjection.le_iff_mul_eq_left.{0} hq hr).mp
      ((TGLV354.CoreProjection.operator_le_iff q (p ⊔ q)).mpr le_sup_right.{0});
  have hRQ :=
    (IsStarProjection.le_iff_mul_eq_right.{0} hq hr).mp
      ((TGLV354.CoreProjection.operator_le_iff q (p ⊔ q)).mpr le_sup_right.{0});
  have hf := IsStarProjection.sub_of_mul_eq_left.{0} hq hr hQR;
  have hsA :=
    of_eq_true
      (Eq.trans.{1}
        (congrFun'.{1, 1}
          (congrArg.{1, 1} Eq.{1}
            (Eq.trans.{1} (star_mul.{0} (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} 1 Q) P)
              (congr.{1, 1}
                (congrArg.{1, 1} HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0}
                  (IsSelfAdjoint.star_eq.{0} (IsStarProjection.isSelfAdjoint.{0} hp)))
                (Eq.trans.{1} (star_sub.{0} 1 Q)
                  (congr.{1, 1}
                    (congrArg.{1, 1} HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0}
                      (star_one.{0} (ContinuousLinearMap.{0, 0, 0, 0} (RingHom.id.{0} ℂ) H H)))
                    (IsSelfAdjoint.star_eq.{0} (IsStarProjection.isSelfAdjoint.{0} hq)))))))
          (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} P (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} 1 Q)))
        (eq_self.{1} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} P (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} 1 Q))));

    Eq.mpr.{0} (id.{0} (congrArg.{1, 1} (fun _a => Eq.{1} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} _a F) _a) hsA))
      (id.{0}
        (Trans.trans.{0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1}
          (Eq.mpr.{0}
            (id.{0}
              (congr.{1, 1}
                (congrArg.{1, 1} Eq.{1}
                  (Eq.trans.{1}
                    (Eq.trans.{1}
                      (congr.{1, 1}
                        (congrArg.{1, 1} HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0}
                          (Eq.trans.{1}
                            (Eq.trans.{1} (congrArg.{1, 1} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} P) (sub_eq_add_neg.{0} 1 Q))
                              (mul_add.{0} P 1 (Neg.neg.{0} Q)))
                            (congr.{1, 1} (congrArg.{1, 1} HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (mul_one.{0} P)) (mul_neg.{0} P Q))))
                        (sub_eq_add_neg.{0} R Q))
                      (mul_add.{0} (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} P (Neg.neg.{0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} P Q))) R (Neg.neg.{0} Q)))
                    (congr.{1, 1}
                      (congrArg.{1, 1} HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0}
                        (Eq.trans.{1} (add_mul.{0} P (Neg.neg.{0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} P Q)) R)
                          (congrArg.{1, 1} (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} P R))
                            (Eq.trans.{1} (neg_mul.{0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} P Q) R)
                              (congrArg.{1, 1} Neg.neg.{0} (mul_assoc.{0} P Q R))))))
